17:T900,- **[01 Quick start](#t.jervk0kndv3r)**: Enter Hubitos, learn the interface and complete your first task.
- **[02 Sign up & sign in](#t.d6896y7jgfxq)**: Register by email, sign in or reset a forgotten password.
- **[03 AI Assistant & image creation](#t.e2i3w47f6f8)**: Choose models, attach files, compare replies and generate images.
- **[04 Commerce images](#t.bxbqbdu9fahn)**: Prepare products, image kits, detail pages, model images and reference-based edits.
- **[05 Commerce video](#t.b9d09ga7oo24)**: Prepare a product and reference, review the script, generate, edit and export a video.
- **[06 Connect social accounts](#t.4n1fe1apklw4)**: Connect a platform account, check plan limits and reconnect when needed.
- **[07 Create & optimize posts](#t.zaegqswiqvdf)**: Create several posts from a topic or improve an existing draft.
- **[08 Edit & publish posts](#t.lp7nx2vc861w)**: Review each platform version, attach media, save drafts and publish.
- **[09 Calendar & post management](#t.7837ohjq20qb)**: Schedule posts, check time zones and resolve publishing failures.
- **[10 Social media analysis](#t.5o56zgpkx78v)**: Analyze public accounts or posts, read reports, export and save results.
- **[11 Trend Radar](#t.kiuhhdw3s1gu)**: Scan a topic, watch relevant trends and turn findings into content ideas.
- **[12 Interaction inbox](#t.t6zg907k90ma)**: Sync interactions, filter them and review a suggested reply before sending.
- **[13 Post performance & interaction insights](#t.i819gyzg4ujc)**: Review post results and turn audience feedback into a content brief.
- **[14 Post-publishing automation](#t.itcngwd6qu96)**: Set a one-time trigger and follow-up action for an X post.
- **[15 Operations tools](#t.pmidd323li2u)**: Use translation, guidelines, calendars, downloads and file-conversion tools.
- **[16 Library & files](#t.17qsvry7j8hi)**: Find and attach files, organize folders and restore saved versions.
- **[17 Settings, plans & credits](#t.j56uxp71hyvc)**: Manage language, desktop features, updates, plans and credits.
- **[18 Workflows & prompts](#t.sso2ypbklcta)**: Follow complete workflows for everyday work, commerce and social content.
